### 1.1 Theme  
**Digital Brain → Physical Body (Embodied Intelligence)**  

### 1.2 Purpose  
Build a full **Docusaurus textbook** on Physical AI & Humanoid Robotics using:  
- Claude Code  
- SpecKit Plus  
- Docusaurus  

Automation must create missing files/folders automatically.

### 1.3 Deliverables  
- Public GitHub repo  
- Live Docusaurus book  

---

## 2. Communication & Language Standards

### 2.1 Languages  
- Code → English  
- Docs → English  
- Technical terms → English  
- File/folder names → English (kebab-case)  
- User-facing responses → Roman Urdu  

### 2.2 Commenting  
- ALL code comments must be English  
- Terminology must stay consistent  

---

## 3. Project Organization Standards

### 3.1 Directory Rules  
- Organize by **feature/module**, not filetype  
- Max 3–4 nesting levels  
- Root must stay clean (configs only)  
- Related files stay together  

### 3.2 Folder Hygiene  
- Delete unused files  
- Use `.gitignore`  
- Keep separate:
  - source code  
  - documentation  
  - configuration  

### 3.3 Documentation Placement  
- Specs → root or `/specs`  
- Textbook content → `/docs`  
- README always updated  

---

## 4. Auto-Create Rules (Claude Code + SpecKit Plus)

### 4.1 Missing Files  
Auto-generate using:

```markdown
# <file-name>
<!-- Auto-generated by Claude Code (SpecKit Plus Rule) -->
```

### 4.2 Missing Folders  
- Auto-create folder  
- Log: `"Auto-created: <folder>"`

### 4.3 Feature Directory Repair  
1. Detect missing files  
2. Create placeholders  
3. Continue execution  

### 4.4 Auto-Generated Files Must Contain  
- Header  
- Comment (auto-gen rule)  
- TODO checklist  

---

## 5. Course Structure (13 Weeks)

### **Module 1 — ROS 2 Nervous System (Weeks 1–5)**
- ROS 2 nodes, topics, services  
- URDF humanoid models  
- rclpy  
- Sensor pipelines  

### **Module 2 — Digital Twin (Weeks 6–7)**
- Gazebo physics  
- Unity viz  
- Multi-sensor simulation  

### **Module 3 — NVIDIA Isaac AI Brain (Weeks 8–10)**
- Isaac Sim  
- Nav2  
- VSLAM  
- Isaac ROS  

### **Module 4 — Vision-Language-Action (Weeks 11–13)**
- Whisper → intent  
- LLM planning  
- VLA pipeline  
- Capstone humanoid  

---

### 6.1 Word Count

- Module 1: 4k–5k  
- Module 2: 3.5k–4.5k

- Module 4: 3.5k–4.5k

- Min 5 examples per module  
- Ubuntu 22.04 compatible  
- Type hints  
- English comments  
- Error handling  

### 6.3 Diagrams  
- 3 per module (total 12)  

### 6.4 Format  
- Docusaurus Markdown  
- Must contain frontmatter

**Branch**: 001-physical-ai-textbook

**Deployment**: Vercel (mandatory)

---

## 1. Summary
This plan details the full implementation strategy for building the **Physical AI & Humanoid Robotics Textbook** using:

- Docusaurus (user installed manually)  
- SpecKit Plus automation  
- Claude Code file generation  
- Vercel deployment  

Scope covers:  
4 modules, 20 examples, 12 diagrams, 15k–20k words.

No backend, no ROS, no hardware, no chatbot development.

---

## 2. Technical Context

### Core Stack
- **Docusaurus v3**
- **Node.js 18+**
- **Markdown / MDX**
- **SpecKit + Claude automation**
- **Ubuntu 22.04** (for testing scripts)

### Deployment
- **VERCEL ONLY** (GitHub Pages not allowed)

### Github
- User creates repo manually  
- User adds remote origin manually  
- Claude Code handles internal file creation only

### Removed (Not required)
- ROS  
- Python environment.yaml  
- NeonDB  
- Backend/frontend structure  
- Any physical robotics execution  
- Any codebase beyond textbook assets

---

## 3. Constitution Compliance Summary
- Language rules ✔  
- Folder structure ✔  
- Auto-create rules ✔  
- Modules/weeks mapping ✔  
- Word count ✔  
- Code examples ✔  
- Diagrams ✔  
- Vercel deployment enforced ✔  
- No hardware/ROS execution ✔  
- Submission alignment ✔  

---

## 4. Project Folder Structure

```
docs/           # All textbook chapters
diagrams/       # 12 diagrams (SVG/PNG)
code/           # 20 runnable examples
examples/       # example metadata (meta.yaml)
scripts/        # CI scripts
static/         # Docusaurus static assets
specs/          # All specifications
.specify/       # SpecKit internal
.claude/        # Claude automation data
templates/      # frontmatter + content templates
history/        # automation logs
CLAUDE.md
```

**Removed:**
- src/
- backend/, frontend/
- api/
- models/, services/
- mobile folders

---

## 5. Implementation Phases

### **Phase 0 — Setup**
- User installs Docusaurus manually  
- Verify folder structure  
- Add required templates  
- Setup SpecKit internal files  

### **Phase 1 — Boilerplate + Templates**
- Create module folders in `docs/`  
- Create example structure in `code/`  
- Add diagram folders  
- Generate meta.yaml templates  
- Add scripts:  
  - check-wordcount.py  
  - link-check.sh  
  - verify.sh  

### **Phase 2 — Content Development**
- Write Module 1–4 content  
- Maintain word count / headings / frontmatter  
- Add concept explanations  
- Follow curriculum (13 weeks)

### **Phase 3 — Examples + Diagrams**
- Add 20 runnable examples  
- Add 12 diagrams  
- Include alt text + captions  

### **Phase 4 — Validation**
- Run wordcount script  
- Run link check  
- Run verify.sh  
- Fix errors before final build

### **Phase 5 — Build & Deploy**
- Build: `npm run build`  
- Deploy to Vercel (only)  
- Validate live version  

### **Phase 6 — Submission**
- Submit GitHub repo link  
- Submit Vercel deployed link  
- Submit WhatsApp contact  

---

specs/001-physical-ai-textbook/

 ├── quickstart.md
 ├── contracts/
 └── tasks.md     # generated by /sp.tasks
```

✔ Single-project structure
